# Agents · T · part 16

1,044 entries, test-runner to test-writer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **test-runner**: Runs the targeted tests for a dw item once (the tests listed in plan.md Proof, plus locked tests and tests for changed areas) and returns a compact pass/fail summary with root-cause notes for failures. Also runs a scoped lint/format check. Stack-agnostic - commands come from the repo profile and the repo's CLAUDE.md. Handles several repos in one run. Never edits files. Use once after simplification and after each fix cycle. ([CroixANI/dw](https://github.com/CroixANI/dw/blob/HEAD/agents/test-runner.md))
- **test-runner**: Runs build, test, and lint commands and returns only failures with minimal log excerpts. Use proactively to keep noisy test output out of the main context. ([ctorres345/Hemisphere](https://github.com/ctorres345/Hemisphere/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-runner.md) · 2 copies)

- **test-runner**: Runs the project's tests, linters and type checks and diagnoses failures. - **test-runner**: Thin runner subagent that executes a test command from a supplied working directory, captures stdout/stderr/exit code, extracts stable suite-native failing-test identifiers into the FAILING_IDENTIFIERS bucket AND records non-reconcilable failures (panics, build errors, crashes with no per-test identifier) separately in the NON_RECONCILABLE_FAILURES bucket, writes a structured artifact, and emits a TEST_RESULT_ARTIFACT marker. Stateless across calls; performs no reconciliation and no pass/fail classification. ([davidsunglee/pi-flow](https://github.com/davidsunglee/pi-flow/blob/HEAD/packages/pi-flow-core/agents/test-runner.md))
